======================================================================
E. CONSTRAINTS (PK/FK/UNIQUE/CHECK)
======================================================================

# alembic_version
   [PK] alembic_version_pkc :: PRIMARY KEY (version_num)
   [n] alembic_version_version_num_not_null :: NOT NULL version_num

# api_keys
   [FK] api_keys_user_id_fkey :: FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
   [PK] api_keys_pkey :: PRIMARY KEY (id)
   [n] api_keys_created_at_not_null :: NOT NULL created_at
   [n] api_keys_id_not_null :: NOT NULL id
   [n] api_keys_is_active_not_null :: NOT NULL is_active
   [n] api_keys_key_hash_not_null :: NOT NULL key_hash
   [n] api_keys_key_prefix_not_null :: NOT NULL key_prefix
   [n] api_keys_name_not_null :: NOT NULL name

   [n] whiteboard_equations_latex_code_not_null :: NOT NULL latex_code

   [n] whiteboard_equations_whiteboard_id_not_null :: NOT NULL whiteboard_id

   [n] whiteboard_equations_y_not_null :: NOT NULL y
